Each path below was created in Step 1 or Step 2, verified against Git history and timestamps. Generated per-page caches are also listed individually.

Pre-existing root files left untouched: README.md and start.ps1 (2026-10-05). Old directories book_xml_backend/book_xml_frontend, .run and .ruff_cache predate Step 1.
